# Route experiments: results

Campaign `results/c1`, replicate `results/c2`. Every contrast in absolute ms with its 95 % rank interval; a contrast is resolvable when its interval excludes zero, its size exceeds the floor of its family and mechanism group, and the replicate agrees on the sign. Relative values are given against the cell's normaliser (the copy route's call at home where the cell has one, else the home variant) and are never summed.
